\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}(g_{1}-g_{2})\cdot\nabla f(g_{1}-\lambda(g_{1}-g_{2}))d\lambda-\int_{0}^{1}(g_{3}-g_{4})\cdot\nabla f(g_{3}-\lambda(g_{3}-g_{4}))d\lambda

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}(g_{3}-g_{4})(g_{3}-\lambda(g_{3}-g_{4}))\nabla^{2}f\left(g_{3}-\lambda(g_{3}-g_{4})-\mu\big((g_{1}-g_{3})-\lambda((g_{1}-g_{3})-(g_{2}-g_{4}))\big)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{1}\left((g_{1}-g_{2})-(g_{3}-g_{4})\right)\cdot\nabla f(g_{1}-\lambda(g_{1}-g_{2}))d\lambda.

Then we obtain (
6.7
) by (
6.3
), (
6.4
) and Lemma
6.5
. This completes the proof of the lemma.

6.2
Estimates of nonlinear terms in the Muskat equation

In this section, we estimate nonlinear terms in the equation (
3.2
).
Denote

\Upsilon(f)=\frac{1}{\langle\partial_{x}f\rangle^{3}},\quad\quad\quad\tilde{\Upsilon}(f_{1},f_{2})=\Upsilon(f_{1})-\Upsilon(f_{2}).

We have the following estimate of
\Upsilon(f)
and
\tilde{\Upsilon}(f_{1},f_{2})
.

Lemma 6.9

For any
0\leq l\leq m+2
, it holds

\displaystyle\|\Upsilon(f)(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{l}}\lesssim t^{-\frac{l}{3}}(1+\|f\|_{T})^{l},

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\Upsilon}(f_{1},f_{2})(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{l}}\lesssim t^{-\frac{l}{3}}\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{T}(1+\|(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{T})^{l}.

for any
t\in(0,T].

The proof follows directly from Lemma
6.8

and the definition of
\|\cdot\|_{T}
in (
1.23
), we omit details here.

For simplicity, we denote the quantity

\displaystyle\mathbf{C}_{n}(f_{1},f_{2}):=\left(\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{T,*}\|(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{T,*}+\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{T}\|(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{T,*}^{2}\right)(1+\|(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{T})^{2n+5}.

Recalling the definition of
\|\cdot\|_{T},\|\cdot\|_{T,*}
and
\|\cdot\|_{X_{T}}
in (
1.23
), we have the following estimate of nonlinear term
\mathsf{N}[f]
.

Lemma 6.10

Let
\mathsf{N}[f](t,x)
be as defined in (
3.1
) with the constant
\varrho_{0}
defined in (
1.22
). For any
T>0
, and any
f,f_{1},f_{2}
with
\|f\|_{X_{T}}+\|f_{1}\|_{X_{T}}+\|f_{2}\|_{X_{T}}<\infty
, the following estimates hold:

•

(i) Nonlinear estimate:

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{m}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{\frac{j+\kappa}{3}}\|\mathsf{N}[f](t)\|_{\dot{C}^{j+\kappa-2}}\lesssim\mathbf{C}_{m}(f,0)+|\varrho_{0}|(T^{\frac{2}{3}}+T^{\frac{1}{10}})\|f\|_{X_{T}}(1+\|f\|_{X_{T}})^{m+5},

(6.8)

•

(ii) Lipschitz type continuity:

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{m}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{\frac{j+\kappa}{3}}

\displaystyle\|\mathsf{N}[f_{1}](t)-\mathsf{N}[f_{2}](t)\|_{\dot{C}^{j+\kappa-2}}

(6.9)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbf{C}_{m}(f_{1},f_{2})+|\varrho_{0}|(T^{\frac{2}{3}}+T^{\frac{1}{10}})||f_{1}-f_{2}||_{X_{T}}(1+||(f_{1},f_{2})||_{X_{T}})^{m+4}.

Proof.
To prove (
6.8
) and (
6.9
), it suffices to show that, for any
n\in\mathbb{N}
,
n\leq m+1
,

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{\frac{n+2}{3}}\|\mathsf{N}[f_{1}](t)-\mathsf{N}[f_{2}](t)\|_{\dot{C}^{n}}

(6.10)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbf{C}_{n}(f_{1},f_{2})+|\varrho_{0}|(T^{\frac{2}{3}}+T^{\frac{1}{10}})||f_{1}-f_{2}||_{X_{T}}(1+||(f_{1},f_{2})||_{X_{T}})^{n+4}.

Note that
\kappa-2\in(0,1)
, then by Lemma
6.5
, the desired estimate (
6.9
) follows from (
6.10
), and (
6.8
) follows from taking
f_{1}=f
,
f_{2}\equiv 0
in (
6.10
) since
\mathsf{N}[0]\equiv 0
.

For simplicity, fix
t\in[0,T]
and omit the time variable in the proof. Denote

\displaystyle\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}f(x)=\partial_{x}f(x)-\Delta_{\alpha}f(x),\ \ \ \ \ \ \mathsf{E}^{\alpha}f(x)=\partial_{x}f(x-\alpha)-\Delta_{\alpha}f(x),

(6.11)

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}[f](x,\alpha)=\frac{\Delta_{\alpha}f(x)\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}f(x)}{\alpha\left\langle\Delta_{\alpha}f(x)\right\rangle^{2}},\ \ \quad\quad\mathsf{M}[f](x)=\frac{\partial_{x}^{2}f(x)}{\left\langle\partial_{x}f(x)\right\rangle^{3}}.

Then we have
\mathsf{N}[f]=\mathsf{N}_{1}[f]+\mathsf{N}_{2}[f]+\varrho_{0}\mathsf{N}_{3}[f]
with

\displaystyle\mathsf{N}_{1}[f](x)=\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathsf{B}[f](\alpha,x)\partial_{x}\mathsf{M}[f](x-\alpha){d\alpha},

\displaystyle\mathsf{N}_{2}[f](x)=-\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{x}^{2}f(x-\alpha)\left(\frac{1}{\left\langle\partial_{x}f(x-\alpha)\right\rangle^{3}}-\frac{1}{\left\langle\partial_{x}f(x)\right\rangle^{3}}\right)\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathsf{N}_{3}[f](x)=-\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathsf{B}[f](\alpha,x)\partial_{x}f(x-\alpha){d\alpha}-\Lambda f(x).

We first consider
\mathsf{N}_{1}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{N}_{1}[f_{2}]
,

\displaystyle\mathsf{N}_{1}[f_{1}](x)-\mathsf{N}_{1}[f_{2}](x)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathsf{B}[f_{1}](\alpha,x)\partial_{x}\left(\mathsf{M}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{M}[f_{2}]\right)(x-\alpha)d\alpha

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}(\mathsf{B}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{B}[f_{2}])(\alpha,x)\partial_{x}\mathsf{M}[f_{2}](x-\alpha)d\alpha

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\mathrm{I}_{1}+\mathrm{I}_{2}.

Integrating by parts gives

\displaystyle\mathrm{I}_{1}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathsf{B}[f_{1}](\alpha,x)\partial_{\alpha}\left(\mathsf{M}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{M}[f_{2}]\right)(x-\alpha)d\alpha

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{\alpha}\mathsf{B}[f_{1}](\alpha,x)\delta_{\alpha}\left(\mathsf{M}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{M}[f_{2}]\right)(x){d\alpha}.

Then, for
n\in\mathbb{N}
,
n\leq m+1
,

\displaystyle\partial_{x}^{n}\mathrm{I}_{1}=-\frac{1}{\pi}\sum_{k+l=n}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{\alpha}\mathsf{B}[f_{1}](\alpha,x)\delta_{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{l}\left(\mathsf{M}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{M}[f_{2}]\right)(x)d\alpha.

(6.12)

Denote
\psi(r)=\frac{r}{\langle r\rangle^{2}}
, then
\mathsf{B}[f](x,\alpha)=\frac{\psi(\Delta_{\alpha}f(x))\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}f(x)}{\alpha}
. Note that
-\partial_{\alpha}(\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}f(x))=\partial_{\alpha}(\Delta_{\alpha}f(x))=\frac{\mathsf{E}^{\alpha}f(x)}{\alpha}
. Hence,

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}\mathsf{B}[f_{1}](\alpha,x)=\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}\left(\psi^{\prime}(\Delta_{\alpha}f(x))\mathsf{E}^{\alpha}f(x)\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}f(x)-\psi(\Delta_{\alpha}f(x))(\mathsf{E}^{\alpha}f(x)+\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}f(x))\right).

This implies

\displaystyle|\partial_{x}^{k}\partial_{\alpha}\mathsf{B}[f](\alpha,x)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}\left(\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3}=k}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\psi^{\prime}(\Delta_{\alpha}f(\cdot))\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{2}}\mathsf{E}^{\alpha}f\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{3}}\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}f\|_{L^{\infty}}\right.

\displaystyle\ \left.+\sum_{k_{4}+k_{5}=k}\|\partial_{x}^{k_{4}}\left(\psi(\Delta_{\alpha}f(\cdot))\right)\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\partial_{x}^{k_{5}}\mathsf{E}^{\alpha}f\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{x}^{k_{5}}\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}f\|_{L^{\infty}})\right).

Recall the definition of
\|\cdot\|_{T}
in (
1.23
). By (
6.3
) in Lemma
6.8
, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\left(\psi(\Delta_{\alpha}f(\cdot))\right)\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{x}^{k_{1}}\left(\psi^{\prime}(\Delta_{\alpha}f(\cdot))\right)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|\Delta_{\alpha}f\|_{\dot{C}^{1}}^{k_{1}}+\|\Delta_{\alpha}f\|_{\dot{C}^{k_{1}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{x}f\|_{\dot{C}^{k_{1}}}+\|\partial_{x}f\|_{\dot{C}^{1}}^{k_{1}}\lesssim t^{-\frac{k_{1}}{3}}\|f\|_{T}(1+\|f\|_{T})^{k_{1}}.

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{k_{3}}\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}f\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}f\|_{\dot{C}^{k_{3}}}\lesssim t^{-\frac{k_{3}}{3}}\|f\|_{T}.

This yields that

\displaystyle|\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{k}\mathsf{B}[f](\alpha,x)|\lesssim\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}\|f\|_{T}(1+\|f\|_{T})^{k+1}\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}=k}t^{-\frac{k_{1}}{3}}(\|\mathsf{E}^{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{k_{2}}f\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{k_{2}}f\|_{L^{\infty}}).

(6.13)

Moreover, by Lemma
6.7
,

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{k_{2}}\mathsf{E}^{\alpha}f\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{x}^{k_{2}}\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}f\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\min\{|\alpha|^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{\dot{C}^{k_{2}+\frac{3}{4}}},|\alpha|^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{x}f\|_{\dot{C}^{k_{2}+\frac{1}{4}}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim t^{-\frac{k_{2}}{3}}\|f\|_{T,*}\min\{(|\alpha|t^{-\frac{1}{3}})^{\frac{3}{4}},(|\alpha|t^{-\frac{1}{3}})^{\frac{1}{4}}\}.

Combining this with (
6.13
), we have

\displaystyle|\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{k}\mathsf{B}[f](\alpha,x)|\lesssim\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}\|f\|_{T,*}\|f\|_{T}(1+\|f\|_{T})^{k+1}t^{-k/3}\min\{(|\alpha|t^{-\frac{1}{3}})^{\frac{3}{4}},(|\alpha|t^{-\frac{1}{3}})^{\frac{1}{4}}\}.

(6.14)

Here and in the following we will use Lemma
6.8
without claim.
For any
0\leq l\leq m+2
, one has

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{l}\left(\mathsf{M}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{M}[f_{2}]\right)\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\partial_{x}^{l}\left({\partial_{x}^{2}(f_{1}-f_{2})\Upsilon(f_{2})}\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}}+\left\|\partial_{x}^{l}\left(\partial_{x}^{2}f_{1}\tilde{\Upsilon}(f_{1},f_{2})\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim t^{-\frac{l+1}{3}}(1+\|(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{T})^{l+1}(\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{T}\|(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{T,*}+\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{T,*}).

Combining this with (
6.14
) and (
6.12
), by interpolation, we derive

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{n}\mathrm{I}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim t^{-\frac{n+1}{3}}\mathbf{C}_{n}(f_{1},f_{2})\int_{\mathbb{R}}\min\{(|\alpha|t^{-\frac{1}{3}})^{\frac{3}{2}},(|\alpha|t^{-\frac{1}{3}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\}\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha^{2}}

(6.15)

\displaystyle\lesssim t^{-\frac{n+2}{3}}\mathbf{C}_{n}(f_{1},f_{2}).

Similarly, integrating by parts, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{I}_{2}=-\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{\alpha}(\mathsf{B}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{B}[f_{2}])(\alpha,x)\delta_{\alpha}\mathsf{M}[f_{2}](x){d\alpha},

and

\displaystyle\partial_{x}^{n}\mathrm{I}_{2}=-\frac{1}{\pi}\sum_{k+l=n}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{k}(\mathsf{B}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{B}[f_{2}])(\alpha,x)\delta_{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{l}\mathsf{M}[f_{2}](x){d\alpha}.

Note that

(\mathsf{B}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{B}[f_{2}])(\alpha,x)=\frac{\Delta_{\alpha}(f_{1}-f_{2})\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}f_{1}}{\alpha\langle\Delta_{\alpha}f\rangle^{2}}+\frac{\Delta_{\alpha}f_{2}\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}(f_{1}-f_{2})}{\alpha\langle\Delta_{\alpha}f\rangle^{2}}+\frac{\Delta_{\alpha}f_{2}\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}f_{2}}{\alpha}\left(\frac{1}{\langle\Delta_{\alpha}f_{1}\rangle^{2}}-\frac{1}{\langle\Delta_{\alpha}f_{2}\rangle^{2}}\right).

Then we can use similar estimates as (
6.13
) to obtain

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{k}(\mathsf{B}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{B}[f_{2}])(\alpha,x)\right|

(6.16)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}\left(\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3}=k}(\|(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{\dot{C}^{k_{1}+1}}+\|(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{\dot{C}^{2}}^{k_{1}})(\|(\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}f_{1},\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}f_{2})\|_{\dot{C}^{k_{2}}}+\|(\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}f_{1},\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}f_{2})\|_{\dot{C}^{1}}^{k_{2}})\right.

\displaystyle\times(\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{k_{3}+1}}+\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{2}}^{k_{3}})

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad+\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}=k}(\|(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{\dot{C}^{k_{1}+1}}+\|(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{\dot{C}^{2}}^{k_{1}})(\|(\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}(f_{1}-f_{2})\|_{\dot{C}^{k_{2}}}+\|(\mathsf{E}_{\alpha}(f_{1}-f_{2})\|_{\dot{C}^{1}}^{k_{2}})\right)

and by definition of
\|\cdot\|_{T}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{k}(\mathsf{B}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{B}[f_{2}])(\alpha,x)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{t^{\frac{k}{3}}\alpha^{2}}(1+\|(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{T})^{k+3}(\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{T}\|(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{T,*}+\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{T,*})\min\{(|\alpha|t^{-\frac{1}{3}})^{\frac{3}{4}},(|\alpha|t^{-\frac{1}{3}})^{\frac{1}{4}}\},

By Lemma
6.8
, we have for any
0\leq l\leq m+2
,

\left\|\partial_{x}^{l}\mathsf{M}[f_{2}]\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim t^{-\frac{l+1}{3}}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}(1+\|f_{2}\|_{T})^{l}.

Hence one has

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{n}\mathrm{I}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim t^{-\frac{n+1}{3}}\mathbf{C}_{n}(f_{1},f_{2})\int_{\mathbb{R}}\min\{(|\alpha|t^{-\frac{1}{3}})^{\frac{3}{2}},(|\alpha|t^{-\frac{1}{3}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\}\frac{d\alpha}{|\alpha|^{2}}

(6.17)

\displaystyle\lesssim t^{-\frac{n+2}{3}}\mathbf{C}_{n}(f_{1},f_{2}).

We conclude from (
6.15
) and (
6.17
) that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{\frac{n+2}{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{n}(\mathsf{N}_{1}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{N}_{1}[f_{2}])(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\mathbf{C}_{n}(f_{1},f_{2}),

(6.18)

for any
0\leq n\leq m+1
.

Then we consider
\mathsf{N}_{2}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{N}_{2}[f_{2}]
, note that

\displaystyle\mathsf{N}_{2}[f](x)=-\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\delta_{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{2}f(x)\delta_{\alpha}\Upsilon(f)(x)\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha^{2}}+\frac{1}{2\pi}\partial_{x}^{2}f(x)\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathcal{O}_{\alpha}\Upsilon(f)(x)\frac{d\alpha}{|\alpha|},

where the operator
\mathcal{O}_{\alpha}
is defined in Lemma
6.7
.
We split
\mathsf{N}_{2}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{N}_{2}[f_{2}]
into

\displaystyle(\mathsf{N}_{2}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{N}_{2}[f_{2}])(x)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\delta_{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{2}(f_{1}-f_{2})(x)\delta_{\alpha}\Upsilon(f_{1})(x)\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha^{2}}-\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\delta_{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{2}f_{2}(x)\delta_{\alpha}\tilde{\Upsilon}(f_{1},f_{2})(x)\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\pi}\partial_{x}^{2}(f_{1}-f_{2})(x)\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathcal{O}_{\alpha}\Upsilon(f_{1})(x)\frac{d\alpha}{|\alpha|}+\frac{1}{2\pi}\partial_{x}^{2}f_{2}(x)\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathcal{O}_{\alpha}\tilde{\Upsilon}(f_{1},f_{2})(x)\frac{d\alpha}{|\alpha|}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{j=1}^{4}\mathrm{II}_{j}(x).

By Lemma
6.7
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{n}\mathrm{II}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\sum_{n_{1}=0}^{n}(\|\partial_{x}^{2}(f_{1}-f_{2})\|_{\dot{C}^{n_{1}+\frac{1}{4}}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}(f_{1}-f_{2})\|_{\dot{C}^{n_{1}+\frac{3}{4}}}\|\Upsilon(f_{1})\|_{\dot{C}^{n-n_{1}+\frac{1}{4}}}\|\Upsilon(f_{1})\|_{\dot{C}^{n-n_{1}+\frac{3}{4}}})^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{n}\mathrm{II}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\sum_{n_{1}=0}^{n}(\|\partial_{x}^{2}f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{n_{1}+\frac{1}{4}}}\|\partial_{x}^{2}f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{n_{1}+\frac{3}{4}}}\|\tilde{\Upsilon}(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{\dot{C}^{n-n_{1}+\frac{1}{4}}}\|\tilde{\Upsilon}(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{\dot{C}^{n-n_{1}+\frac{3}{4}}})^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{n}\mathrm{II}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\sum_{n_{1}=0}^{n}\|\partial_{x}^{2}(f_{1}-f_{2})\|_{\dot{C}^{n_{1}}}\|\Upsilon(f_{1})\|_{\dot{C}^{n-n_{1}+\frac{3}{4}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Upsilon(f_{1})\|_{\dot{C}^{n-n_{1}+\frac{5}{4}}}^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{n}\mathrm{II}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\sum_{n_{1}=0}^{n}\|\partial_{x}^{2}f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{n_{1}}}\|\tilde{\Upsilon}(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{\dot{C}^{n-n_{1}+\frac{3}{4}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\tilde{\Upsilon}(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{\dot{C}^{n-n_{1}+\frac{5}{4}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Combining this with Lemma
6.9
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{\frac{n+2}{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{n}(\mathsf{N}_{2}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{N}_{2}[f_{2}])(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\mathbf{C}_{n}(f_{1},f_{2}).

(6.19)

Finally, we estimate lower order terms

\displaystyle\mathsf{N}_{3}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{N}_{3}[f_{2}]=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathsf{B}[f_{1}](\alpha,x)\partial_{x}(f_{1}-f_{2})(x-\alpha){d\alpha}-\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}(\mathsf{B}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{B}[f_{2}])(\alpha,x)\partial_{x}f_{2}(x-\alpha){d\alpha}

\displaystyle-\Lambda(f_{1}-f_{2})(x)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\mathrm{III}_{1}+\mathrm{III}_{2}+\mathrm{III}_{3}.

Using integration by parts,

\displaystyle|\partial_{x}^{n}\mathrm{III}_{1}|\lesssim\sum_{k+l=n}\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{k}\mathsf{B}[f_{1}](\alpha,x)||\delta_{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{l}(f_{1}-f_{2})(x)|d\alpha,

\displaystyle|\partial_{x}^{n}\mathrm{III}_{2}|\lesssim\sum_{k+l=n}\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\partial_{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{k}(\mathsf{B}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{B}[f_{2}])(\alpha,x)||\delta_{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{l}f_{2}(x)|d\alpha.

Then we use (
6.13
), (
6.16
), together with the facts that

\displaystyle|\delta_{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{l}f(x)|\lesssim|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{2}}\|f\|_{\dot{C}^{l+\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{1}_{l\geq 1}t^{-\frac{1}{3}(l-\frac{1}{2})}\|f\|_{T}+\mathbf{1}_{l=0}\|f\|_{X_{T}}),

to obtain

\displaystyle|\partial_{x}^{n}\mathrm{III}_{1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k+l=n}\|f_{1}\|_{T,*}\|f_{1}\|_{T}^{2}(1+\|f_{1}\|_{T})^{k}t^{-\frac{k}{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\min\{|\alpha|^{\frac{3}{4}}t^{-\frac{1}{4}},|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{4}}t^{-\frac{1}{12}}\}|\delta_{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{l}(f_{1}-f_{2})(x)|\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f_{1}\|_{T,*}\|f_{1}\|_{T}^{2}(1+\|f_{1}\|_{T})^{n}t^{-\frac{n}{3}}(\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{T}+t^{-\frac{1}{6}}||f_{1}-f_{2}||_{X_{T}}).

\displaystyle|\partial_{x}^{n}\mathrm{III}_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k+l=n}(1+\|(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{T})^{k+4}\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{T}t^{-\frac{k}{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\min\{|\alpha|^{\frac{3}{4}}t^{-\frac{1}{4}},|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{4}}t^{-\frac{1}{12}}\}|\delta_{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{l}f_{2}(x)|\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\|(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{T})^{n+4}\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{T}t^{-\frac{n}{3}}(\|f_{2}\|_{T}+t^{-\frac{1}{6}}||f_{2}||_{X_{T}}).

For
\mathrm{III}_{3}
, there holds

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{n}\mathrm{III}_{3}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim t^{-\frac{n}{3}}||f_{1}-f_{2}||_{X_{T}}.

We conclude that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{\frac{n}{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{n}(\mathsf{N}_{3}[f_{1}]-\mathsf{N}_{3}[f_{2}])(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim(T^{\frac{2}{3}}+T^{\frac{1}{10}})||f_{1}-f_{2})||_{X_{T}}(1+||(f_{1},f_{2})||_{X_{T}})^{n+5}.

Combining this with (
6.18
) and (
6.19
) yields (
6.10
). This completes the proof of the lemma.

Lemma 6.11

Let
\mathsf{G}[f,\phi]
be as defined in (
3.5
). For any
T>0
, and any
f,f_{1},f_{2}
with
\|f\|_{X_{T}}+\|f_{1}\|_{X_{T}}+\|f_{2}\|_{X_{T}}<\infty
, we have the following estimates:

•

(i) Nonlinear estimate:

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{m}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{\frac{n+\kappa}{3}}\|\mathsf{G}[f_{1},f_{2}](t)\|_{\dot{C}^{n+\kappa-2}}\lesssim\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{T}\|f_{1}\|_{T,*}(1+\|(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{T})^{m+5},

•

(ii) Lipschitz type continuity:

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{m}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{\frac{n+\kappa}{3}}\|(\mathsf{G}[f_{1},\phi]-\mathsf{G}[f_{2},\phi])(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{n+\kappa-2}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\lesssim(\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{T}\|f_{1}\|_{T,*}+\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{T,*}\|f_{2}-\phi\|_{T})(1+\|(f_{1},f_{2},\phi)\|_{T})^{m+5}.

Proof.
Note that

\displaystyle\mathsf{G}[f_{1},f_{2}]=\Upsilon(f_{2},f_{1})\Lambda^{3}f_{1},\quad\quad\quad\mathsf{G}[f_{1},\phi]-\mathsf{G}[f_{2},\phi]

\displaystyle=\mathsf{G}[f_{1},f_{2}]+\tilde{\Upsilon}(\phi,f_{2})\Lambda^{3}(f_{1}-f_{2}).

For any fixed
t\in[0,T]
, by Lemma
6.9
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\partial_{x}^{n}\mathsf{G}[f_{1},f_{2}](t)\right\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-2}}\lesssim t^{-\frac{n+\kappa}{3}}\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{T}\|f_{1}\|_{T,*}(1+\|(f_{1},f_{2})\|_{T})^{m+5},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\partial_{x}^{n}\left(\tilde{\Upsilon}(\phi,f_{2})\Lambda^{3}(f_{1}-f_{2})\right)(t)\right\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-2}}\lesssim t^{-\frac{n+\kappa}{3}}\|f_{2}-\phi\|_{T}\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{T,*}(1+\|(f_{2},\phi)\|_{T})^{m+5}.

Then we complete the proof.

Remark 6.12

Following the proof of Lemma
6.11
, we obtain the following
L^{\infty}
estimates,

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\mathsf{G}[f_{1},f_{2}](t)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{T}\|f_{1}\|_{T,*},

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(\mathsf{G}[f_{1},\phi]-\mathsf{G}[f_{2},\phi])(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{T}\|f_{1}\|_{T,*}+\|f_{1}-f_{2}\|_{T,*}\|f_{2}-\phi\|_{T}.

6.3
Estimates of nonlinear terms in 2D Peskin problem

The following part is devoted to estimate the nonlinear terms of the Peskin equation. In this subsection, we fix
m\in\mathbb{N}
,
\kappa\in(0,1)
and
1-\kappa\ll 1
. Recall the definition of semi-norms

\displaystyle\|h\|_{T}=\sup_{t\in[0,T]}(\|\partial_{x}h(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}+t^{m+\kappa}\|\partial_{x}h(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{m+\kappa}}),

\displaystyle\|h\|_{T,*}=\sup_{t\in[0,T]}(t^{\frac{1}{10}}\|\partial_{x}h(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{10}}}+t^{m+\kappa}\|\partial_{x}h(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{m+\kappa}}),

and the set
\mathcal{X}^{\sigma}_{T,\Phi}
defined in (
4.14
) with
\sigma\in(0,1)
. We have the following results.

Lemma 6.13

Let
\mathcal{N}(X)
be as defined in (
4.4
). For any
T\in(0,1)
, and any functions
X,Y,Z
satisfying
\|X\|_{T}+\|Y\|_{T}+\|Z\|_{T}<\infty
, and

\displaystyle\mathfrak{M}_{T}:=\mathfrak{M}_{T}(X)+\mathfrak{M}_{T}(Y)+\mathfrak{M}_{T}(Z)<\infty,

(6.20)

where
\mathfrak{M}_{T}(X)
is defined in (
4.18
), the following estimates hold:

•

(i) Nonlinear estimate:

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0,m}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{j+\kappa}\|\mathcal{N}(X)(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{j+\kappa}}\lesssim\mathfrak{M}_{T}\|X\|_{T,*}^{2}(1+\|X\|_{T})^{2(m+1)}.

(6.21)

•

(ii) Lipschitz type continuity:

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0,m}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{j+\kappa}

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{N}(Y)(t)-\mathcal{N}(Z)(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{j+\kappa}}

(6.22)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathfrak{M}_{T}\|Y-Z\|_{T}(\|(Y,Z)\|_{T,*}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|(Y,Z)\|_{T})(1+\|(Y,Z)\|_{T})^{2(m+1)}.

The following lemma shows the estimates for
\mathsf{M}(\partial_{x}X)
.

Lemma 6.14

Let
\mathsf{M}(\partial_{x}X)
be as defined in (
4.8
). For any
T>0
, and any
X,Y,Z
satisfying
\|X\|_{T}+\|Y\|_{T}+\|Z\|_{T}<\infty
and (
6.20
), the following estimates hold:

•

(i) Nonlinear estimate:

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0,m}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{j+\kappa}\|\mathsf{M}(\partial_{x}X)(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{j+\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim\mathfrak{M}_{T}\|X\|_{T,*}^{2}(1+\|X\|_{T})^{m+5}.

•

(ii) Lipschitz type continuity:

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0,m}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{j+\kappa}\|(\mathsf{M}(\partial_{x}Y)-\mathsf{M}(\partial_{x}Z))(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{j+\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\lesssim\mathfrak{M}_{T}(\|Y-Z\|_{T,*}+\|Y-Z\|_{T}\|(Y,Z)\|_{T,*})\|(Y,Z)\|_{T,*}(1+\|(Y,Z)\|_{T})^{m+4}.

Recalling the definition (
4.5
), we first prove the following lemmas.

Lemma 6.15

[
25
, Lemma 2.4]

For any function
f,g:{\mathbb{R}}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
, denote
\tilde{g}(\alpha)={\Delta}_{\alpha}g(0)
. Then for any
\sigma\in(0,1)
and
0<\varepsilon<10^{-3}\min\{1-\sigma,\sigma\}
, there holds

\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}f(\alpha)(\partial_{\alpha}\tilde{g})(\alpha)d\alpha\right|\lesssim\|f\|_{\dot{C}^{\sigma+\varepsilon}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|f\|_{\dot{C}^{\sigma-\varepsilon}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\dot{C}^{1-\sigma+\varepsilon}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\dot{C}^{1-\sigma-\varepsilon}}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Lemma 6.16

For any function
f:\mathbb{S}\to\mathbb{R}
, then for any
0<\gamma_{1}\leq\gamma<1
,

\displaystyle\sup_{x}\sup_{\alpha\neq h}\frac{|\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}f(x)-\tilde{\Delta}_{h}f(x)||\alpha|^{\gamma-\gamma_{1}}}{|\alpha-h|^{\gamma}}\lesssim{\|f^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma_{1}}}}.

Proof.
Consider
x=0
without loss of generality, and denote
\tilde{f}(\alpha)=\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}f(0)
.
Thanks to the periodicity, it is enough to consider
\alpha\in(0,\pi)
and
h\in[\alpha/2,\alpha/2+2\pi]
.
We have

\displaystyle\tilde{f}(\alpha)-\tilde{f}(h)=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{2}\cot\left(\frac{h}{2}\right)-\frac{1}{h}\right)\int_{0}^{-h}f^{\prime}(\omega)d\omega-\left(\frac{1}{2}\cot\left(\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)-\frac{1}{\alpha}\right)\int_{0}^{-\alpha}f^{\prime}(\omega)d\omega

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{h}\int_{0}^{-h}f^{\prime}(\omega)d\omega-\frac{1}{\alpha}\int_{0}^{-\alpha}f^{\prime}(\omega)d\omega

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{2}\cot\left(\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)-\frac{1}{\alpha}\right)\int_{-\alpha}^{-h}f^{\prime}(\omega)d\omega+\left(\frac{1}{2}\cot\left(\frac{h}{2}\right)-\frac{1}{h}-\left(\frac{1}{2}\cot\left(\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)-\frac{1}{\alpha}\right)\right)\int_{0}^{-h}f^{\prime}(\omega)d\omega

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{h}\int_{-\alpha}^{-h}(f^{\prime}(\omega)-f^{\prime}(-\alpha))d\omega+\frac{\alpha-h}{\alpha h}\int_{0}^{-\alpha}(f^{\prime}(\omega)-f^{\prime}(-\alpha))d\omega.

Here, with a slight abuse of notation, we denote
\int_{x}^{y}g(\omega)d\omega=-\int_{y}^{x}g(\omega)d\omega
if
y<x
.
Note that

\left|\frac{1}{2}\cot\left(\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)-\frac{1}{\alpha}\right|\lesssim|\alpha|,\ \ \ \ \ \left|\frac{1}{2}\cot\left(\frac{\alpha}{2}\right)-\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}\cot\left(\frac{h}{2}\right)+\frac{1}{h}\right|\lesssim|\alpha-h|.

Hence,

\displaystyle|\tilde{f}(\alpha)-\tilde{f}(h)|\lesssim\left(|h|+|\alpha|+\frac{|h-\alpha|^{\gamma_{1}}}{|h|}+\frac{|\alpha|^{\gamma_{1}}}{|h|}\right)|h-\alpha|\|f^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma_{1}}}.

We obtain

\displaystyle\frac{|\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}f-\tilde{\Delta}_{h}f|}{|\alpha-h|^{\gamma}}\lesssim\left(|h|+|\alpha|+\frac{|h-\alpha|^{\gamma_{1}}}{|h|}+\frac{|\alpha|^{\gamma_{1}}}{|h|}\right)|h-\alpha|^{1-\gamma}\|f^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma_{1}}}\lesssim\frac{\|f^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma_{1}}}}{|h|^{\gamma-\gamma_{1}}}\lesssim\frac{\|f^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma_{1}}}}{|\alpha|^{\gamma-\gamma_{1}}}.

This completes the proof of the lemma.

Fix
m\in\mathbb{N}
,
\kappa\in(0,1)
and
1-\kappa\ll 1
. For
f(t,x,\alpha):[0,T]\times\mathbb{S}\times\mathbb{S}\to\mathbb{R}
, we define

\displaystyle[f_{1}]_{T,*}:=\sup_{\vartheta\in[\frac{1}{2},\kappa]}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}(t^{\vartheta-\frac{1}{4}}\|f_{1}(t)\|_{\vartheta,\frac{1}{4}}+t^{m+\vartheta-\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{x}^{m}f_{1}(t)\|_{\vartheta,\frac{1}{4}})

(6.23)

\displaystyle+\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\sup_{x}(t^{\frac{1}{10}}\|f_{1}(t,x,\cdot)\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{10}}}+t^{m+\kappa}\|\partial_{x}^{m}f_{1}(t,x,\cdot)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}}),

\displaystyle[f_{1}]_{T}:=\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\sup_{\alpha}(\|f_{1}(t,\cdot,\alpha)\|_{L^{\infty}}+t^{m+\kappa}\|f_{1}(t,\cdot,\alpha)\|_{\dot{C}^{m+\kappa}})+[f_{1}]_{T,*},

where

\displaystyle\|f_{1}(t)\|_{a,b}:=\sup_{\alpha,z\in\mathbb{S}}|\alpha|^{b}\left(\|f_{1}(t,\cdot,\alpha)\|_{\dot{C}^{a}}+\frac{\|f_{1}(t,\cdot,\alpha)-f_{1}(t,\cdot,\alpha-z)\|_{L^{\infty}}}{|z|^{a}}\right),\ \ \ \text{for}\ a,b\in[0,1].

(6.24)

Denote

\displaystyle\interleave h\interleave_{T}=\sup_{t\in[0,T]}(\|h(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}+t^{m+\kappa}\|h(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{m+\kappa}}),

\displaystyle\interleave h\interleave_{T,*}=\sup_{t\in[0,T]}(t^{\frac{1}{10}}\|h(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{10}}}+t^{m+\kappa}\|h(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{m+\kappa}}).

Note that
\interleave\partial_{x}h\interleave_{T}=\|h\|_{T}
and
\interleave\partial_{x}h\interleave_{T,*}=\|h\|_{T,*}
, where
\|\cdot\|_{T}
and
\|\cdot\|_{T,*}
are defined in (
4.13
).
We have the following lemma.

Lemma 6.17

Let
f_{1}:[0,T]\times\mathbb{S}\times\mathbb{S}\to\mathbb{R}
, and
f_{2},f_{3}:[0,T]\times\mathbb{S}\to\mathbb{R}
satisfy

\displaystyle[f_{1}]_{T}+\|f_{2}\|_{T}+\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T}<\infty

with
0<T<1
.
Then for

\displaystyle f(t,x)=\int_{\mathbb{S}}f_{1}(t,x,\alpha)\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}(t,x)f_{3}(t,x-\alpha)\frac{d\alpha}{\tilde{\alpha}},

where
\tilde{\alpha}
and
\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}
are defined in (
4.5
),
there holds

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{m+\kappa}\|f(t,\cdot)\|_{\dot{C}^{m+\kappa}}\lesssim\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}([f_{1}]_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T}+[f_{1}]_{T}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}[f_{1}]_{T}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T}).

(6.25)

If we further have
\int_{\mathbb{S}}f_{1}(t,x,\alpha)\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}(t,x)\frac{d\alpha}{\tilde{\alpha}}=0
, then

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{m+\kappa}\|f(t,\cdot)\|_{\dot{C}^{m+\kappa}}\lesssim[f_{1}]_{T}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}.

(6.26)

Proof.
We first prove (
6.25
). For simplicity, for any
t\in[0,T]
, we fix
t
and drop the time variable
t
in this proof, and denote
f_{i}^{m_{i}}=\partial_{x}^{m_{i}}f_{i}
for
i=1,2,3
,
m_{i}\in\mathbb{N}
. We have

\displaystyle\partial_{x}^{m}f(x)=\sum_{m_{1}+m_{2}+m_{3}=m}\int_{\mathbb{S}}f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x,\alpha)\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}^{m_{2}}(x)f_{3}^{m_{3}}(x-\alpha)\frac{d\alpha}{\tilde{\alpha}}.

In the following, we drop the summation for
m_{1}+m_{2}+m_{3}=m
with a slight abuse of notation.

For any
\beta\neq 0
, we can write

\displaystyle\delta_{\beta}\partial_{x}^{m}f(x)=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{S}}\delta_{\beta}f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x,\alpha)\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}^{m_{2}}(x)f_{3}^{m_{3}}(x-\alpha)\frac{d\alpha}{\tilde{\alpha}}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{S}}f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x-\beta,\alpha)\delta_{\beta}\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}^{m_{2}}(x)f_{3}^{m_{3}}(x-\alpha)\frac{d\alpha}{\tilde{\alpha}}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{S}}f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x-\beta,\alpha)\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}^{m_{2}}(x-\beta)\delta_{\beta}f_{3}^{m_{3}}(x-\alpha)\frac{d\alpha}{\tilde{\alpha}}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle P_{1}+P_{2}+P_{3}.

By the definition of
\|\cdot\|_{a,b}
in (
6.24
), we have

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\delta_{\beta}f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x,\alpha)|\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}\|f_{1}^{m_{1}}\|_{\kappa,\frac{1}{4}}.

Hence it is easy to check that

\displaystyle|P_{1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}t^{-(m_{1}+\kappa-\frac{1}{4})}[f_{1}]_{T,*}\|f_{3}^{m_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{\mathbb{S}}|\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}^{m_{2}}|\frac{d\alpha}{|\alpha|^{\frac{5}{4}}}

(6.27)

\displaystyle\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}t^{-(m+\kappa)}[f_{1}]_{T,*}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T}.

Then we deal with
P_{2}
. Observe that

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}^{m_{2}}(x)=\alpha\partial_{\alpha}(\Delta_{\alpha}f_{2}^{m_{2}})(x)+\delta_{\alpha}f_{2}^{m_{2}}(x)\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\tilde{\alpha}}\right).

(6.28)

Hence, by (
4.2
),

\displaystyle P_{2}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x-\beta,\alpha)\partial_{\alpha}(\Delta_{\alpha}\delta_{\beta}f_{2}^{m_{2}})(x)f_{3}^{m_{3}}(x-\alpha){d\alpha}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}}f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x-\beta,\alpha)(\delta_{\alpha}\delta_{\beta}f_{2}^{m_{2}})(x)f_{3}^{m_{3}}(x-\alpha)\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\tilde{\alpha}}\right)\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle P_{2,1}+P_{2,2}.

Since
|\frac{1}{\alpha}(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\tilde{\alpha}})|\lesssim\min\{1,|\alpha|^{-2}\}
for
|\alpha|\leq\pi
, we remark that
P_{2,1}
is the main term, and
P_{2,2}
is the remainder error term.
Applying Lemma
6.15
with
f(\alpha)=f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x,\alpha)f_{3}^{m_{3}}(x-\alpha)
,
g=\delta_{\beta}f_{2}^{m_{2}}
, one has

\displaystyle|P_{2,1}|\lesssim\prod_{+,-}\left\{\|\delta_{\beta}f_{2}^{m_{2}}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{4}\pm\varepsilon}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{x}\|f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x,\alpha)f_{3}^{m_{3}}(x-\alpha)\|_{\dot{C}_{\alpha}^{\frac{3}{4}\pm\varepsilon}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\}.

Here we denote
\dot{C}^{b}_{\alpha}
the Hölder semi-norm in variable
\alpha
. By Lemma
6.5
, it is easy to check that for
\gamma=\frac{3}{4}\pm\varepsilon
,

\displaystyle\|f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x,\alpha)f_{3}^{m_{3}}(x-\alpha)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}\dot{C}_{\alpha}^{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f_{1}^{m_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}\dot{C}^{\gamma}_{\alpha}}\|f_{3}^{m_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|f_{1}^{m_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\alpha}}\|f_{3}^{m_{3}}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim t^{-(m_{1}+m_{3})-\gamma}([f_{1}]_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T}+[f_{1}]_{T}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}).

Hence we obtain that

\displaystyle|P_{2,1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}\prod_{+,-}\left\{t^{-(m_{1}+m_{3})-\frac{3}{4}\mp\varepsilon}\|\partial_{x}f_{2}^{m_{2}}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{3}{4}\pm\varepsilon}}\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}([f_{1}]_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T}+[f_{1}]_{T}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*})

\displaystyle\lesssim t^{-\kappa-m}|\beta|^{\kappa}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}([f_{1}]_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T}+[f_{1}]_{T}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}),

(6.29)

where
m=m_{1}+m_{2}+m_{3}
.
For
P_{2,2}
, by (
4.2
) we have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}h(\alpha)\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\tilde{\alpha}}\right)\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}h(\alpha)\left(\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}-\frac{\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|\leq\pi}}{\tilde{\alpha}^{2}}\right)d\alpha\right|

(6.30)

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|h(\alpha)\right|\min\{1,|\alpha|^{-2}\}d\alpha.

Then we obtain that

\displaystyle|P_{2,2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f_{1}^{m_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|f_{3}^{m_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\|\delta_{\alpha}\delta_{\beta}f_{2}^{m_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\min\{1,|\alpha|^{-2}\}d\alpha

\displaystyle\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}\|f_{1}^{m_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|f_{3}^{m_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}f_{2}^{m_{2}}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim t^{-\kappa-m+\frac{1}{2}}|\beta|^{\kappa}[f_{1}]_{T}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T}.

Combining this with (
6.29
) to obtain that for any
t\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle t^{m+\kappa}|\beta|^{-\kappa}|P_{2}|\lesssim\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}([f_{1}]_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T}+[f_{1}]_{T}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}[f_{1}]_{T}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T}).

(6.31)

Finally, we deal with
P_{3}
. If
m_{1}+m_{2}\neq 0
,
i.e.

m_{3}<m
, we directly have

\displaystyle|P_{3}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f_{1}^{m_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{m_{2}+\frac{3}{4}}}\|\delta_{\beta}f_{3}\|_{\dot{C}^{m_{3}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}t^{-(m+\kappa)}[f_{1}]_{T}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}.

If
m_{1}+m_{2}=0
, we observe that
for any function
h_{1},h_{2}
, by a change of variable,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{S}}h_{1}(\alpha)\delta_{\beta}h_{2}(x-\alpha)d\alpha

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{S}}\left(h_{1}(\alpha)-h_{1}(\alpha-\beta)\right)h_{2}(x-\alpha)d\alpha

\displaystyle=-\int_{\mathbb{S}}\left(h_{1}(\alpha)-h_{1}(\alpha-\beta)\right)\delta_{\alpha}h_{2}(x)d\alpha.

Applying this to
P_{3}
, it follows

\displaystyle P_{3}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{S}}\left(\frac{f_{1}(x-\beta,\alpha)\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}(x-\beta)}{\alpha}-\frac{f_{1}(x-\beta,\alpha-\beta)\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha-\beta}f_{2}(x-\beta)}{\alpha-\beta}\right)\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m}(x)d\alpha

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{S}}{(f_{1}(x-\beta,\alpha)-f_{1}(x-\beta,\alpha-\beta))\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}(x-\beta)}\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m}(x)\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\int_{\mathbb{S}}f_{1}(x-\beta,\alpha-\beta)(\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}-\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha-\beta}f_{2})(x-\beta)\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m}(x)\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\int_{\mathbb{S}}f_{1}(x-\beta,\alpha-\beta)\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha-\beta}f_{2}(x-\beta)\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m}(x)\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\alpha-\beta}\right)d\alpha

\displaystyle:=P_{3,1}+P_{3,2}+P_{3,3}.

For
P_{3,1}
, by (
6.24
) and Lemma
6.7
we have

\displaystyle|f_{1}(x-\beta,\alpha)-f_{1}(x-\beta,\alpha-\beta)|\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}\min\left\{\sup_{x}\|f_{1}(x,\cdot)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}},|\alpha|^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|f_{1}\|_{\kappa,\frac{1}{4}}\right\},

\displaystyle|\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}(x,\beta)|\lesssim|\alpha|^{a}\|\partial_{x}f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{a}},\quad\quad|\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m}(x)|\lesssim|\alpha|^{a}\|f_{3}\|_{\dot{C}^{m+a}},\ \ \forall a\in(0,1).

(6.32)

This implies

\displaystyle|P_{3,1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{S}}|(f_{1}(x-\beta,\alpha)-f_{1}(x-\beta,\alpha-\beta))\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}(x-\beta)\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m}(x)|\frac{d\alpha}{|\alpha|}

(6.33)

\displaystyle\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}\int_{\mathbb{S}}\min\left\{\sup_{x}\|f_{1}(x,\cdot)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}},|\alpha|^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|f_{1}\|_{\kappa,\frac{1}{4}}\right\}\frac{d\alpha}{|\alpha|^{\frac{4}{5}}}\|\partial_{x}f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{10}}}\|f_{3}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{10}+m}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}t^{-(m+\kappa)}[f_{1}]_{T,*}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}.

For
P_{3,2}
, observe that

\displaystyle({\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}}-{\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha-\beta}f_{2}})(x)

\displaystyle=\partial_{x}(\delta_{-\beta}f_{2})(x-\alpha)-(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}f_{2}(x)-\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha-\beta}f_{2}(x))

\displaystyle=\partial_{\alpha}(\alpha\Delta_{\alpha}\delta_{-\beta}f_{2})(x)-(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}f_{2}(x)-\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha-\beta}f_{2}(x))

\displaystyle=\alpha\partial_{\alpha}(\Delta_{\alpha}\delta_{-\beta}f_{2})(x)+\Delta_{\alpha}\delta_{-\beta}f_{2}(x)-(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}f_{2}(x)-\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha-\beta}f_{2}(x)).

We further decompose
P_{3,2}
into

\displaystyle P_{3,2}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{S}}f_{1}(x-\beta,\alpha-\beta)\partial_{\alpha}(\Delta_{\alpha}\delta_{-\beta}f_{2})(x)\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m}(x){d\alpha}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{S}}f_{1}(x-\beta,\alpha-\beta)(\Delta_{\alpha}\delta_{-\beta}f_{2})(x)\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m}(x)\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{S}}f_{1}(x-\beta,\alpha-\beta)(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}f_{2}-\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha-\beta}f_{2})(x)\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m}(x)\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle P_{3,2,1}+P_{3,2,2}+P_{3,2,3}.

We estimate
P_{3,2,1}
similarly as
P_{2,1}
, which yields

\displaystyle|P_{3,2,1}|\lesssim t^{-\kappa-m}|\beta|^{\kappa}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}([f_{1}]_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T}+[f_{1}]_{T}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}).

(6.34)

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle|P_{3,2,2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim[f_{1}]_{T}\int_{\mathbb{S}}|\delta_{\alpha}\delta_{-\beta}f_{2}(x)\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m}(x)|\frac{d\alpha}{|\alpha|^{2}}.

By Lemma
6.7
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\delta_{\alpha}\delta_{-\beta}f_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}\min\left\{|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{x}f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{3}{4}}},|\alpha|^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\partial_{x}f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{4}}}\right\},

\displaystyle\|\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\min\left\{|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{4}}\|f_{3}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{4}+m}},|\alpha|^{\frac{3}{4}}\|f_{3}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{3}{4}+m}}\right\}.

It follows

\displaystyle|P_{3,2,2}|

(6.35)

\displaystyle\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}\|f_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{\mathbb{S}}\min\left\{\|\partial_{x}f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{3}{4}}},|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{x}f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{4}}}\right\}\min\{\|f_{3}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{4}+m}},|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{2}}\|f_{3}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{3}{4}+m}}\}\frac{d\alpha}{|\alpha|^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}\|f_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\partial_{x}f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{3}{4}}}\|\partial_{x}f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{4}}}\|f_{3}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{4}+m}}\|f_{3}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{3}{4}+m}})^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Finally, similar to
P_{3,1}
, by Lemma
6.16
,

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{4}}\frac{\|\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}f_{2}-\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha-\beta}f_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}}{|\beta|^{\kappa}}\lesssim\|\partial_{x}f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{4}}}.

Hence,

\displaystyle|P_{3,2,3}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{S}}|f_{1}(x-\beta,\alpha-\beta)(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}f_{2}-\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha-\beta}f_{2})(x)\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m}(x)|\frac{d\alpha}{|\tilde{\alpha}|}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}\|f_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\alpha}}\|\partial_{x}f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{4}}}\int_{\mathbb{S}}|\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m}|\frac{d\alpha}{|\tilde{\alpha}|^{\frac{5}{4}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim t^{-{m+\kappa}}|\beta|^{\kappa}[f_{1}]_{T}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}.

Combining this with (
6.34
) and (
6.35
), we deduce that

\displaystyle t^{m+\kappa}|\beta|^{-\kappa}|P_{3,2}|\lesssim[f_{1}]_{T}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}.

(6.36)

Then we estimate

P_{3,3}=-\int_{\mathbb{S}}f_{1}(x-\beta,\alpha-\beta)\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha-\beta}f_{2}(x-\beta)\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m}(x)\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\alpha-\beta}\right)d\alpha.

By (
6.32
), one has

\displaystyle|P_{3,3}|\lesssim\|f_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\alpha}}\|\partial_{x}f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}}\|f_{3}\|_{\dot{C}^{m+\frac{\kappa}{2}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\alpha-\beta|^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}|\alpha|^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}\left|\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\alpha-\beta}\right|d\alpha.

Note that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\alpha-\beta|^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}|\alpha|^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}\left|\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\alpha-\beta}\right|d\alpha\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\alpha-1|^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}|\alpha|^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}\left|\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\alpha-1}\right|d\alpha\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}.

This yields that

\displaystyle|P_{3,3}|\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}\|f_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,\alpha}}\|\partial_{x}f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}}\|f_{3}\|_{\dot{C}^{m+\frac{\kappa}{2}}}\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}t^{-(m+\kappa)}[f_{1}]_{T}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}.

(6.37)

Combining (
6.33
), (
6.36
), and (
6.37
), we deduce

\displaystyle t^{m+\kappa}|\beta|^{-\kappa}|P_{3}|\lesssim[f_{1}]_{T}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}.

(6.38)

Hence we obtain from (
6.27
), (
6.31
) and (
6.38
) that

\displaystyle\|\delta_{\beta}\partial_{x}f^{m}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim|P_{1}|+|P_{2}|+|P_{3}|

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}t^{-(m+\kappa)}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}([f_{1}]_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T}+[f_{1}]_{T}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}[f_{1}]_{T}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T}).

This completes the proof of (
6.25
).

Then we prove (
6.26
). If

\int_{\mathbb{S}}f_{1}(t,x,\alpha)\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}(t,x)\frac{d\alpha}{\tilde{\alpha}}=0

holds, then

\displaystyle f(t,x)=-\int_{\mathbb{S}}f_{1}(t,x,\alpha)\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}(t,x)\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}(t,x)\frac{d\alpha}{\tilde{\alpha}}.

We note the finite difference operator in
f_{3}
allows
f_{3}
to share derivative, hence we avoid the endpoint norm
\|f_{3}\|_{L^{\infty}}
in the estimate (
6.26
). The idea of the proof is similar to that of (
6.25
). For
m\in\mathbb{N}
, we write

\displaystyle\partial_{x}^{m}f(x)=-\sum_{m_{1}+m_{2}+m_{3}=m}\int_{\mathbb{S}}f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x,\alpha)\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}^{m_{2}}(x)\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m_{3}}(x)\frac{d\alpha}{\tilde{\alpha}}.

We drop the summation for
m_{1}+m_{2}+m_{3}=m
with a slight abuse of notation.

For any
\beta\neq 0
, we can write

\displaystyle\delta_{\beta}\partial_{x}^{m}f(x)=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{S}}\delta_{\beta}f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x,\alpha)\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}^{m_{2}}(x)\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m_{3}}(x)\frac{d\alpha}{\tilde{\alpha}}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{S}}f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x-\beta,\alpha)\delta_{\beta}\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}^{m_{2}}(x)\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m_{3}}(x)\frac{d\alpha}{\tilde{\alpha}}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{S}}f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x-\beta,\alpha)\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}f_{2}^{m_{2}}(x-\beta)\delta_{\beta}\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m_{3}}(x)\frac{d\alpha}{\tilde{\alpha}}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\tilde{P}_{1}+\tilde{P}_{2}+\tilde{P}_{3}.

Note that

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{4}}|\delta_{\beta}f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x,\alpha)|\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}\|f_{1}^{m_{1}}\|_{\kappa,\frac{1}{4}}.

Then it is straightforward to obtain

\displaystyle|\tilde{P}_{1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}t^{-(m_{1}+\kappa-\frac{1}{4})}[f_{1}]_{T,*}\|f_{2}^{m_{2}+1}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\int_{\mathbb{S}}|\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m_{3}}|\frac{d\alpha}{|\alpha|^{\frac{9}{8}}}

(6.39)

\displaystyle\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}t^{-(m+\kappa)}[f_{1}]_{T,*}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}.

Then we deal with
P_{2}
. By (
6.28
), we can write

\displaystyle\tilde{P}_{2}=

\displaystyle\int f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x-\beta,\alpha)\partial_{\alpha}(\Delta_{\alpha}\delta_{\beta}f_{2}^{m_{2}})(x)\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m_{3}}(x){d\alpha}

\displaystyle+\int f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x-\beta,\alpha)(\delta_{\alpha}\delta_{\beta}f_{2}^{m_{2}})(x)\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m_{3}}(x)\left(\frac{1}{\alpha}-\frac{1}{\tilde{\alpha}}\right)\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\tilde{P}_{2,1}+\tilde{P}_{2,2}.

By Lemma
6.15
, one has

\displaystyle|\tilde{P}_{2,1}|\lesssim\prod_{+,-}\left\{\|\delta_{\beta}f_{2}^{m_{2}}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{4}\pm\varepsilon}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{x}\|f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x,\alpha)\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m_{3}}(\tau)\|_{\dot{C}_{\alpha}^{\frac{3}{4}\pm\varepsilon}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\}.

Here we use
\dot{C}^{a}_{\alpha}
denote the
\dot{C}^{a}
Hölder semi-norm in variable
\alpha
. By Lemma
6.5
, we obtain that for
\gamma=\frac{3}{4}\pm\varepsilon
,

\displaystyle\frac{|f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x,\alpha)\delta_{\alpha}f_{3}^{m_{3}}(x)-f_{1}^{m_{1}}(x,\alpha-z)\delta_{\alpha-z}f_{3}^{m_{3}}(x)|}{|z|^{\gamma}}\lesssim\|f_{1}^{m_{1}}\|_{\gamma,\frac{1}{4}}\|f_{3}^{m_{3}}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{4}}}+\|f_{1}^{m_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|f_{3}^{m_{3}}\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}.

Hence we obtain that

\displaystyle|\tilde{P}_{2,1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}\prod_{+,-}\left\{\|\partial_{x}f_{2}^{m_{2}}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{3}{4}\pm\varepsilon}}\left(\|f_{1}^{m_{1}}\|_{\frac{3}{4}\pm\varepsilon,\frac{1}{4}}\|f_{3}^{m_{3}}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{4}}}+\|f_{1}^{m_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|f_{3}^{m_{3}}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{3}{4}\pm\varepsilon}}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim t^{-\kappa-m}|\beta|^{\kappa}[f_{1}]_{T}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}.

(6.40)

For
\tilde{P}_{2,2}
, by (
6.30
),

\displaystyle|\tilde{P}_{2,2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f_{1}^{m_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|f_{3}^{m_{3}}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\|\delta_{\alpha}\delta_{\beta}f_{2}^{m_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{4}}\min\{1,|\alpha|^{-2}\}d\alpha

\displaystyle\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}\|f_{1}^{m_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|f_{3}^{m_{3}}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\|f_{2}^{m_{2}}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa+\frac{1}{4}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\min\{|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{2}},|\alpha|^{-\frac{3}{2}}\}d\alpha

\displaystyle\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}\|f_{1}^{m_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|f_{3}^{m_{3}}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\|f_{2}^{m_{2}}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa+\frac{1}{4}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim t^{-\kappa-m}|\beta|^{\kappa}[f_{1}]_{T}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}.

Combining this with (
6.40
), we obtain that for any
t\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle t^{m+\kappa}|\beta|^{-\kappa}|\tilde{P}_{2}|\lesssim[f_{1}]_{T}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}.

(6.41)

Finally, for
\tilde{P}_{3}
, we consider two cases.
If
m_{1}+m_{2}\neq 0
, we directly have

\displaystyle|\tilde{P}_{3}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f_{1}^{m_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}f_{2}\|_{\dot{C}^{m_{2}+\frac{3}{4}}}\|f_{3}\|_{\dot{C}^{m_{3}+\kappa+\frac{1}{4}}}\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}t^{-(m+\kappa)}[f_{1}]_{T}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}.

When
m_{1}=m_{2}=0
, by the condition
\int f_{1}(x,\alpha)E^{\alpha}f_{2}(x)\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha}=0
, the estimate is the same as
P_{3}
. Hence,

\displaystyle t^{m+\kappa}|\beta|^{-\kappa}|\tilde{P}_{3}|\lesssim[f_{1}]_{T}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}.

(6.42)

Hence we obtain from (
6.39
), (
6.41
) and (
6.42
) that

\displaystyle\|\delta_{\beta}\partial_{x}f^{m}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim|\tilde{P}_{1}|+|\tilde{P}_{2}|+|\tilde{P}_{3}|\lesssim|\beta|^{\kappa}t^{-(m+\kappa)}[f_{1}]_{T}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}.

This holds for any
\beta\neq 0
. Then we complete the proof of (
6.26
).

Proof of Lemma
6.13
.

In this proof, we consider for any fixed
t\in[0,T]
. We first prove (
6.21
). Rewrite (
4.4
) to the following form

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}(X)(x)=

\displaystyle-\sum\int_{\mathbb{R}}H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}X(x))\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}X_{i}(x)(\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}X|)\partial_{x}X_{j})(x-\alpha)\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha}.

where the sum is for some
i,j=1,2
and
H(x)=C_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}}\frac{x_{i_{1}}x_{i_{2}}x_{i_{3}}}{|x|^{4}}
,
i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}=1,2
. With a slight abuse of notation, we omit the summation symbol and subscript
i,j
in our proof, and we do not distinguish
\partial_{x}f
and
f^{\prime}
.
Note that
H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}X(x))\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}X_{i}(x)=\partial_{\alpha}G(\delta_{\alpha}X(x))-\frac{1}{2}\cot\frac{\alpha}{2}
, hence

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{S}}H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}X(t,x))\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}X_{i}\frac{d\alpha}{\tilde{\alpha}}=0.

By (
4.1
), and applying (
6.26
) with

\displaystyle f_{1}(t,x,\alpha)=H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}X(t,x)),\ \ \ \ f_{2}(t,x)=X_{i}(t,x),\ \ \ \ f_{3}(t,x)=\big(\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}X|)\partial_{x}X_{j}\big)(t,x),

we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{m+\kappa}\|\mathcal{N}(X)(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{m+\kappa}}\lesssim[f_{1}]_{T}\|f_{2}\|_{T,*}\interleave f_{3}\interleave_{T,*}.

Denote
\tilde{H}[X](t,x,\alpha)=H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}X)(t,x)
. We first consider

\displaystyle[f_{1}]_{T}=[\tilde{H}[X](t)]_{T},

with
[\cdot]_{T}
defined in (
6.23
). For any
k\in\mathbb{N}
,
\gamma\in[\frac{1}{2},\kappa]
,

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{k}\tilde{H}[X](t))\|_{\gamma,\frac{1}{4}}=\sup_{\alpha,z\in\mathbb{S}}|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{k}H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}X(t,\cdot))\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}+\frac{\|\partial_{x}^{k}H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}X(t,.))-\partial_{x}^{k}H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha-z}X(t,.))\|_{L^{\infty}}}{|z|^{\gamma}}\right).

(6.43)

By definition, it is easy to check that for any
0\leq k\leq m
,
0\leq t\leq T
,

\sup_{\alpha\in\mathbb{S}}|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{x}^{k}H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}X(t,\cdot))\|_{\dot{C}^{\gamma}}\lesssim t^{-(k+\gamma-\frac{1}{4})}(1+\mathbf{\Theta}_{X}(T))^{m+2}\|X\|_{T,*}(1+\|X\|_{T})^{m+1}.

(6.44)

Moreover, by Lemma
6.16
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\alpha,z\in\mathbb{S}}|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{4}}\frac{\|\partial_{x}^{k}H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}X(t,x))-\partial_{x}^{k}H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha-z}X(t,x))\|_{L^{\infty}}}{|z|^{\gamma}}

(6.45)

\displaystyle\lesssim t^{-(k+\gamma-\frac{1}{4})}(1+\mathbf{\Theta}_{X}(T))^{m+2}\|X\|_{T,*}(1+\|X\|_{T})^{m+1}.

Combine (
6.43
), (
6.44
) with (
6.45
), we obtain

\displaystyle[\tilde{H}[X](t)]_{T,*}\lesssim(1+\mathbf{\Theta}_{X}(T))^{m+2}\|X\|_{T,*}(1+\|X\|_{T})^{m+1}.

(6.46)

Note that
\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}X|)\partial_{x}X_{j}=\mathcal{T}(|\partial_{x}X|)\frac{\partial_{x}X_{j}}{|\partial_{x}X|}
, then for any
k\in\mathbb{N}
,
a\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}X|)\partial_{x}X_{j}\|_{\dot{C}^{k+a}}\lesssim\mathfrak{C}(\|X\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}\dot{W}^{1,\infty}})t^{-(k+a)}\mathbf{\Theta}_{X}(T)\|X\|_{T,*}(1+\|X\|_{T})^{k+1}.

This yields

\displaystyle\interleave\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}X|)\partial_{x}X_{j}\interleave_{T,*}\lesssim\mathfrak{C}(\|X\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}\dot{W}^{1,\infty}})\mathbf{\Theta}_{X}(T)\|X\|_{T,*}(1+\|X\|_{T})^{m+1}.

Then we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}(t^{\kappa}\|\mathcal{N}(X)(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}}+t^{m+\kappa}\|\mathcal{N}(X)(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{m+\kappa}})\lesssim\mathfrak{M}_{T}\|X\|_{T,*}^{2}(1+\|X\|_{T})^{2(m+1)}.

This completes the proof of (
6.21
).

Then we deal with (
6.22
).
Note that

\displaystyle(\mathcal{N}(Z)-\mathcal{N}(Y))(x)=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}(H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}Y)-H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}Z))(x)\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}Y(x)(\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)\partial_{x}Y)(x-\alpha)\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}}H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}Z)(x)\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}(Y-Z)(x)(\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)\partial_{x}Y)(x-\alpha)\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}}H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}Z)(x)\tilde{\mathsf{E}}^{\alpha}Z(x)(\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)\partial_{x}Y_{j}-\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Z|)\partial_{x}Z_{j})(x-\alpha)\frac{d\alpha}{\alpha}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle B_{1}+B_{2}+B_{3}.

We apply (
6.25
) with
(f_{1},f_{2},f_{3})
equal to
((H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}Y)-H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}Z))(x),Y(x),\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)\partial_{x}Y(x))
,
(H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}Z)(x),(Y-Z)(x),\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)\partial_{x}Y(x))
and
(H(\tilde{\Delta}_{\alpha}Z)(x),Z(x),(\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)\partial_{x}Y-\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Z|)\partial_{x}Z)(x))
, respectively. Then we derive

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{m+\kappa}\|\partial_{x}^{m}B_{1}(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\|Y\|_{T,*}\left([(\tilde{H}[Y]-\tilde{H}[Z])]_{T,*}\interleave\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)\partial_{x}Y\interleave_{T}+[(\tilde{H}[Y]-\tilde{H}[Z])]_{T}\interleave\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)\partial_{x}Y\interleave_{T,*}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\left.+T^{\frac{1}{2}}[(\tilde{H}[Y]-\tilde{H}[Z])]_{T}\interleave\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)\partial_{x}Y\interleave_{T}\right),

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{m+\kappa}\|\partial_{x}^{m}B_{2}(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\|W\|_{T,*}\left([\tilde{H}[Z]]_{T,*}\interleave\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)\partial_{x}Y\interleave_{T}+[\tilde{H}[Z]]_{T}\interleave\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)\partial_{x}Y\interleave_{T,*}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}[\tilde{H}[Z]]_{T}\interleave\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)\partial_{x}Y\interleave_{T}\right),

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{m+\kappa}\|\partial_{x}^{m}B_{3}(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\|Z\|_{T,*}\left([\tilde{H}[Z]]_{T,*}\interleave(\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)\partial_{x}Y-\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Z|)\partial_{x}Z)\interleave_{T}+[\tilde{H}[Z]]_{T}\interleave(\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)\partial_{x}Y-\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Z|)\partial_{x}Z)\interleave_{T,*}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\left.+T^{\frac{1}{2}}[\tilde{H}[Z]]_{T}\|Z\|_{T,*}\interleave(\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)\partial_{x}Y-\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Z|)\partial_{x}Z)\interleave_{T}\right).

Denote
W=Y-Z
. We prove similarly as (
6.46
) that

\displaystyle[(\tilde{H}[Y]-\tilde{H}[Z])]_{T,*}\lesssim(1+\mathbf{\Theta}_{Y}(T)+\mathbf{\Theta}_{Z}(T))^{m+1}\|W\|_{T}(1+\|(Y,Z)\|_{T})^{m+1},

and

\displaystyle\interleave\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)\partial_{x}Y-\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Z|)\partial_{x}Z\interleave_{T,*}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\lesssim\mathfrak{C}(\|(Y,Z)\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}\dot{W}^{1,\infty}})(1+\mathbf{\Theta}_{Y}(T)+\mathbf{\Theta}_{Z}(T))\|W\|_{T}(1+\|(Y,Z)\|_{T})^{m+1}.

Combining the above estimates to obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{m+\kappa}

\displaystyle(\|\partial_{x}^{m}B_{1}(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}}+\|\partial_{x}^{m}B_{2}(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}}+\|\partial_{x}^{m}B_{3}(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}})

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathfrak{M}_{T}\|W\|_{T}(\|(Y,Z)\|_{T,*}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|(Y,Z)\|_{T})(1+\|(Y,Z)\|_{T})^{2(m+1)}.

This completes the proof of (
6.22
).

Proof of Lemma
6.14
.
The proof of
(i)
follows the proof of
(ii)
with
(Y,Z)=(X,0)
, so we only need to prove
(ii)
. For simplicity, we only estimate the norm
\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{\kappa}\|(\mathsf{M}(\partial_{x}Y)-\mathsf{M}(\partial_{x}Z))(t)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}
. The estimates for higher order derivatives are parallel. We denote
W=Y-Z
,
\vec{V}=(Y,Z)
,
W^{\prime}=\partial_{x}(Y-Z)
and
\vec{V}^{\prime}=(\partial_{x}Y,\partial_{x}Z)
in this proof. During the proof, we fix
t\in[0,T]
and drop the time variable
t
. Note that

\displaystyle\mathsf{M}(\partial_{x}Y)(x)-\mathsf{M}(\partial_{x}Z)(x)=-\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathfrak{R}(\alpha,s)\frac{d\alpha}{|\alpha|^{\frac{3}{2}}}.

Here we denote

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}(\alpha,s)=\delta_{\alpha}(\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)-\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Z|))(x)\delta_{\alpha}\partial_{x}Y(x)+\delta_{\alpha}(\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Z|))(x)\delta_{\alpha}\partial_{x}W(x)

\displaystyle\ \ \quad\quad\quad+\partial_{x}W(x)D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Y)(x)+\partial_{x}Z(x)(D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Y)-D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Z))(x)

with

\displaystyle D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(A)=\delta_{\alpha}(\mathbf{T}(|A|))-\delta_{\alpha}A\cdot\nabla\big(\mathbf{T}(|A|)\big),\ \ \ \forall\ A:\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{R}^{2}.

By Lemma
6.5
,

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{R}(\alpha,\cdot)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)-\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Z|)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\|\delta_{\alpha}\partial_{x}Y\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta_{\alpha}(\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)-\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Z|))\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}Y\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}

(6.47)

\displaystyle+\|\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Z|)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\|\delta_{\alpha}\partial_{x}W\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta_{\alpha}(\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Z|))\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{x}W\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{x}W\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\|D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Y)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{x}W\|_{L^{\infty}}\|D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Y)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{x}Z\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\|(D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Y)-D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Z))\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{x}Z\|_{L^{\infty}}\|(D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Y)-D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Z))\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}},

where all
\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}
denote Hölder semi-norm of
x
variable. We remark that all the estimates related to the tension
\mathbf{T}
and its derivatives will result in a factor
\mathfrak{M}_{T}
. We omit this factor in the following to avoid redundancy.
Lemma
6.8
implies that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Y|)-\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Z|)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim\|W^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}+\|W^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}},

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{T}(|\partial_{x}Z|)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim\|Z^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}},\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \|D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Y)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\delta_{\alpha}Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2},

\displaystyle\|D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Y)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\delta_{\alpha}Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\|\delta_{\alpha}Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\|\delta_{\alpha}Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2},

\displaystyle\|(D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Y)-D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Z))\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\delta_{\alpha}W^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\delta_{\alpha}\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta_{\alpha}\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|W^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}},

\displaystyle\|(D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Y)-D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Z))\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\|\delta_{\alpha}W^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\|\delta_{\alpha}\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\delta_{\alpha}W^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\delta_{\alpha}\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}+\|\delta_{\alpha}W^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\delta_{\alpha}\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\|\delta_{\alpha}\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\|\delta_{\alpha}\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|W^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\delta_{\alpha}\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}(\|W^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}+\|W^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}),

where all the norms are defined with respect to
x
variable. Hence,

\displaystyle\|D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Y)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\min\{|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{5}}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{10}}}^{2},|\alpha|\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}\}\lesssim\min\{|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{5}}t^{-\frac{1}{5}},|\alpha|t^{-1}\}\|Y\|_{T,*}^{2}\|Y\|_{T}.

\displaystyle\|D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Y)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim\min\{|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{5}}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{5}}}(1+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}),|\alpha|(\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\|Y^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{2})\}

\displaystyle\lesssim t^{-(\kappa-\frac{1}{2})}\min\{|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{10}}t^{-\frac{1}{10}},|\alpha|t^{-1}\}\|Y\|_{T,*}^{2}(1+\|Y\|_{T})^{2},

\displaystyle\|(D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Y)-D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Z))\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\min\left\{|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{5}}\|W^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{10}}}^{2}(1+\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}),|\alpha|(\|W^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}\|W^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}})\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\min\{|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{5}}t^{-\frac{1}{5}},|\alpha|t^{-1}\}(\|\vec{V}\|_{T,*}\|W\|_{T}+\|W\|_{T,*})\|\vec{V}\|_{T,*}(1+\|\vec{V}\|_{T}).

Similarly, we have both

\displaystyle\|(D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Y)-D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Z))\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{5}}\big(\|W^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{5}}}+\|W^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{5}}}\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\big)(1+\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}})+|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{5}}\|W^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{5}}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{5}}t^{-(\kappa-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{5})}(\|\vec{V}\|_{T,*}\|W\|_{T}+\|W\|_{T,*})\|\vec{V}\|_{T,*}(1+\|\vec{V}\|_{T}),

and

\displaystyle\|(D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Y)-D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Z))\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim|\alpha|\left\{\|W^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}}\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\|W^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}}(1+\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}})\right.

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\left.+\|W^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}}\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}(\|W^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}+\|W^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\vec{V}^{\prime}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}})\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim|\alpha|t^{-(\kappa+\frac{1}{2})}(\|\vec{V}\|_{T,*}\|W\|_{T}+\|W\|_{T,*})\|\vec{V}\|_{T,*}(1+\|\vec{V}\|_{T})^{2}.

These imply

\displaystyle\|(D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Y)-D_{\mathbf{T},\alpha}(\partial_{x}Z))\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim\frac{\min\{|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{5}}t^{-\frac{1}{5}},|\alpha|t^{-1}\}}{t^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}(\|\vec{V}\|_{T,*}\|W\|_{T}+\|W\|_{T,*})\|\vec{V}\|_{T,*}(1+\|\vec{V}\|_{T})^{2}.

(6.48)

Combining (
6.47
)-(
6.48
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{R}(\alpha,\cdot)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim\frac{\min\{|\alpha|^{\frac{1}{5}}t^{-\frac{1}{5}},|\alpha|t^{-1}\}}{t^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}(\|\vec{V}\|_{T,*}\|W\|_{T}+\|W\|_{T,*})\|\vec{V}\|_{T,*}(1+\|\vec{V}\|_{T})^{2}.

This yields

\displaystyle\|\mathsf{M}(\partial_{x}Y)-\mathsf{M}(\partial_{x}Z)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}}\|\mathfrak{R}(\alpha,\cdot)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa-\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{d\alpha}{|\alpha|^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim t^{-\kappa}(\|\vec{V}\|_{T,*}\|W\|_{T}+\|W\|_{T,*})\|\vec{V}\|_{T,*}(1+\|\vec{V}\|_{T})^{2}.

This completes the proof of the lemma.

6.4
Estimates of nonlinear terms in 3D Peskin problem

The following lemma establishes the boundedness of non-convolution-type singular integral operators and will be useful for estimating the 3D Peskin problem. While the proof follows classical methods, we refer interested readers to
[
2
]
for a detailed exposition.

Lemma 6.18

Consider a function
\mathbf{G}:\mathbb{R}^{2}\to\mathbb{R}
. Suppose that there exists
C_{\mathbf{G}}>0
such that

\displaystyle|\mathbf{G}(\theta,\eta)|+|\theta-\eta||\nabla_{\theta,\eta}\mathbf{G}(\theta,\eta)|+|\theta-\eta|^{2}|\nabla_{\theta}\nabla_{\eta}\mathbf{G}(\theta,\eta)|\leq\frac{C_{\mathbf{G}}}{|\theta-\eta|},\ \ \ \forall\theta,\eta\in\mathbb{R}^{2},\ \theta\neq\eta.

(6.49)

Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}f(\theta)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\mathbf{G}(\theta,\eta)\nabla\cdot f(\eta)d\eta.

Then for
\kappa\in(0,1)
, there holds

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{M}f\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}}\lesssim C_{\mathbf{G}}\|f\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}}.

Remark 6.19

The result of Lemma
6.18
on
\mathbb{R}^{2}
can be extended parallel to
\mathbb{S}^{2}
. More precisely, for
\mathbf{G}:\mathbb{S}^{2}\to\mathbb{R}
, denote

\tilde{\mathcal{M}}F(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}})=\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\mathbf{G}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}},\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}})\widetilde{\nabla}\cdot F(\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}})d\mu_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}).

Suppose there exists
C_{\mathbf{G}}>0
such that

|\mathbf{G}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}},\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}})|+|\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}||\widetilde{\nabla}_{\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}},\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}}\mathbf{G}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}},\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}})|+|\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}|^{2}|\widetilde{\nabla}_{\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}}}\widetilde{\nabla}_{\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}}\mathbf{G}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}},\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}})|\leq\frac{C_{\mathbf{G}}}{|\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}|},

then for
\kappa\in(0,1)
,

\|\tilde{\mathcal{M}}F\|_{C^{\kappa}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}\lesssim C_{\mathbf{G}}\|F\|_{C^{\kappa}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}.

To prove this, we can work on local charts of
\mathbb{S}^{2}
and reduce the problem onto
\mathbb{R}^{2}
, we omit the details of proof here.

We now estimate the nonlinear term
N_{1}(F,\Phi)
, introduced in (
5.6
), in appropriate time-weighted Hölder norms. This will be crucial for establishing the local well-posedness via a fixed-point argument in the space
\mathcal{Z}_{T,\Phi}^{\sigma}
. For simplicity, in the following of this section we will denote

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}(X;\Phi)=\mathfrak{M}_{1}\|X-\Phi\|_{Z_{T}}(\|X-\Phi\|_{Z_{T}}+T^{\kappa}M_{\Phi})(1+\|X-\Phi\|_{Z_{T}}+m_{\Phi}+T^{\kappa}M_{\Phi})^{m+3},

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}(Y,Z;\Phi)=\mathfrak{M}_{1}\|Y-Z\|_{Z_{T}}(\|(Y-\Phi,Z-\Phi)\|_{Z_{T}}+T^{\varepsilon}M_{\Phi})(1+\|(Y-\Phi,Z-\Phi)\|_{Z_{T}}+m_{\Phi}+T^{\kappa}M_{\Phi})^{m+3}.

where
M_{\Phi}
and
m_{\Phi}
are defined in (
5.19
),
\|\cdot\|_{Z_{T}}
is defined in (
5.24
), and
\mathfrak{M}_{1}
is defined in (
5.25
). The following lemma provides both growth and Lipschitz-type bounds, with explicit dependence on the profile
\Phi
.

Lemma 6.20

Let
N_{1}(F,\Phi)
be as defined in (
5.6
). For any
T\in(0,1)
, and any functions
X,Y,Z\in\mathcal{Z}_{T,\Phi}^{\sigma}
, the following estimates hold:

•

(i) Nonlinear estimate:

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0,m}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{j+\kappa}\|N_{1}(X,\Phi)(t)\|_{C^{j+\kappa}}\lesssim\mathcal{M}(X;\Phi),

•

(ii) Lipschitz-type continuity:

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0,m}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{j+\kappa}\|(N_{1}(Y,\Phi)-N_{1}(Z,\Phi))(t)\|_{C^{j+\kappa}}\lesssim\mathcal{M}(Y,Z;\Phi).

Proof.
For simplicity, we fix
t\in[0,T]
and omit the time variable in the proof. We start with the lowest order Hölder estimate.
Let

\tilde{G}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}},\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}):=G(X(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}})-X(\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}))-G(\Phi(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}})-\Phi(\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}})).

By (
5.8
), the condition (
6.49
) holds. Let
C_{\tilde{G}}=C(1+\mathbf{\Theta}_{0})^{m}\|\widetilde{\nabla}(X-\Phi)\|_{L^{\infty}}(1+\|\widetilde{\nabla}(X-\Phi)\|_{L^{\infty}})
. Applying Remark
6.19
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\|N_{1}\|_{C^{\kappa}}\lesssim\mathfrak{M}_{1}\|\widetilde{\nabla}(X-\Phi)\|_{L^{\infty}}(1+\|\widetilde{\nabla}(X-\Phi)\|_{L^{\infty}})\|(\mathbf{T}(|\widetilde{\nabla}X|)\widetilde{\nabla}X)\|_{C^{\kappa}}\lesssim t^{-\kappa}\mathcal{M}(X;\Phi).

(6.50)

For higher order derivatives of
N_{1}(X,\Phi)
, we have

N_{1}(X,\Phi)(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}})=-\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\tilde{G}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}},\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}})\widetilde{\nabla}\cdot(\mathbf{T}(|\widetilde{\nabla}X|)\widetilde{\nabla}X)(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}})d\mu_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}),

By (
5.20
), it holds

\tilde{\nabla}_{\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}}}^{k}\tilde{G}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}},\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}})=\sum_{l\leq k}C(k,l)\tilde{\nabla}_{\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}}^{k-l}G_{l}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}},\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}),

where

G_{l}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}},\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}})=(\widetilde{\nabla}_{\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}}}+\widetilde{\nabla}_{\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}})^{l}\tilde{G}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}},\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}})
.
Using the fact that

\displaystyle|G_{l}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}},\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}})|+|\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}||\widetilde{\nabla}_{\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}},\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}}G_{l}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}},\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}})|

\displaystyle+|\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}|^{2}|\widetilde{\nabla}_{\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}}}\widetilde{\nabla}_{\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}}G_{l}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}},\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}})|

\displaystyle\lesssim t^{-l}(1+\mathbf{\Theta}_{0})^{l}\frac{\|\widetilde{\nabla}(X-\Phi)\|_{T}(1+\|\widetilde{\nabla}(X-\Phi)\|_{T}+TM_{\Phi})^{m}}{|\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}|},

and applying Remark
6.19
with corresponding
G_{l}
and
C_{G_{l}}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|N_{1}\|_{C^{m+\kappa}}\lesssim t^{-m-\kappa}\mathcal{M}(X;\Phi).

(6.51)

Then we estimate
N_{1}(Y,\Phi)-N_{1}(Z,\Phi)
. Note that

\displaystyle N_{1}(Y,\Phi)(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}})-N_{1}(Z,\Phi)(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}})

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\left(G(Y(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}})-Y(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}))-G(Z(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}})-Z(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}))\right)\widetilde{\nabla}\cdot(\mathbf{T}(|\widetilde{\nabla}Y|)\widetilde{\nabla}Y)(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}})d\mu_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}})

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\left(G(Z(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}})-Z(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}))-G(\Phi(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}})-\Phi(\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}))\right)\widetilde{\nabla}\cdot(\mathbf{T}(|\widetilde{\nabla}Y|)\widetilde{\nabla}Y-\mathbf{T}(|\widetilde{\nabla}Z|)\widetilde{\nabla}Z)(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}})d\mu_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}).

Applying Remark
6.19
with
\mathbf{G}=G(Y(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}})-Y(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}))-G(Z(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}})-Z(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}))
and
\mathbf{G}=G(t,Z(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}})-Z(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}))-G(\Phi(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}})-\Phi(\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}))
, respectively, and by similar methods of (
6.50
) and (
6.51
), we deduce

\displaystyle\|(N_{1}(Y,\Phi)-N_{1}(Z,\Phi))\|_{C^{j+\kappa}}\lesssim t^{-j+\kappa}\mathcal{M}(Y,Z;\Phi),

for
j=0,m
. This completes the proof.

Lemma 6.21

Let
N_{2}(F,\Phi)
be defined in (
5.6
). For any
T\in(0,1)
, and any
X,Y,Z\in\mathcal{Z}_{T,\Phi}^{\sigma}
, we have the following estimates:

•

1: Nonlinear estimate:

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0,m}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{j+\kappa}\|N_{2}(X,\Phi)(t)\|_{C^{j+\kappa}}\lesssim\mathcal{M}(X;\Phi),

•

2: Lipschitz type continuity:

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0,n}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}t^{j+\kappa}\|(N_{2}(Y,\Phi)-N_{2}(Z,\Phi))(t)\|_{C^{j+\kappa}}\lesssim\mathcal{M}(Y,Z;\Phi).

Proof.
The proof is just similar with Lemma
6.20
. Fix
t\in[0,T]
and for simplicity we drop
t
in the proof. We denote

R(X,\Phi)=\mathbf{T}(|\widetilde{\nabla}X|)\widetilde{\nabla}X-\mathbf{T}(|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|)\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi-J(\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi)\widetilde{\nabla}(X-\Phi).

By definition, we have

\displaystyle R(X,\Phi)=R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3},

with

\displaystyle R_{1}=(\mathbf{T}(|\widetilde{\nabla}X|)-\mathbf{T}(|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|))\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi-\mathbf{T}^{\prime}(|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|)(|\widetilde{\nabla}X|-|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|)\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi,

\displaystyle R_{2}=(\mathbf{T}(|\widetilde{\nabla}X|)-\mathbf{T}(|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|))\widetilde{\nabla}(X-\Phi),

\displaystyle R_{3}=\mathbf{T}^{\prime}(|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|)(|\widetilde{\nabla}X|-|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|)\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi-J_{2}(\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi)\widetilde{\nabla}(X-\Phi),

with
J_{2}
defined in (
5.5
). For lowest order Hölder norm, let
\mathbf{G}=G(\Phi(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}})-\Phi(\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}))
and
C_{\mathbf{G}}=m_{\Phi}(1+m_{\Phi})
. By (
5.8
), the condition (
6.49
) is satisfied. Then we apply Remark
6.19
to obtain

\|N_{2}(X,\Phi)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}}\lesssim(1+m_{\Phi})\|R(X,\Phi)\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}}.

By Newton-Leibniz formula, we can rewrite the remainder terms as

\displaystyle R_{1}=\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\mathbf{T}^{\prime\prime}(|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|+\lambda\mu(|\widetilde{\nabla}X|-|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|))\lambda(|\widetilde{\nabla}X|-|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|)^{2}\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi d\mu d\lambda,

\displaystyle R_{2}=\int_{0}^{1}\mathbf{T}^{\prime}(|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|+\lambda(|\widetilde{\nabla}X|-|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|))(|\widetilde{\nabla}X|-|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|)\widetilde{\nabla}(X-\Phi)d\lambda,

\displaystyle R_{3}=\mathbf{T}^{\prime}(|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|)\left(\frac{\widetilde{\nabla}(X-\Phi):\widetilde{\nabla}(X-\Phi)}{|\widetilde{\nabla}X|+|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|}+\left(\frac{2\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi:\widetilde{\nabla}(X-\Phi)}{|\widetilde{\nabla}X|+|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|}-\frac{\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi:\widetilde{\nabla}(X-\Phi)}{|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|}\right)\right)\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi.

By Lemma
6.5
and Lemma
6.8
, we can see that

\|R(X,\Phi)\|_{C^{\kappa}}\lesssim t^{-\kappa}\mathfrak{M}_{1}\|X-\Phi\|_{Z_{T}}(\|X-\Phi\|_{Z_{T}}+T^{\kappa}M_{\Phi})(1+\|X-\Phi\|_{Z_{T}}+m_{\Phi}),

which infers

\|N_{2}(X,\Phi)\|_{C^{\kappa}}\lesssim t^{-\kappa}\mathcal{M}(X;\Phi).

For higher order derivatives of
N_{2}(X,\Phi)
, we only need to use (
5.20
), integrating by parts and taking derivatives on
R
, then by similar way of (
6.51
), we can obtain

\displaystyle\|N_{2}(X,\Phi)\|_{C^{m+\kappa}}\lesssim t^{-m-\kappa}\mathcal{M}(X;\Phi).

For
N_{2}(Y,\Phi)-N_{2}(Z,\Phi)
, we will shortly denote
\vec{V}=(Y,Z)
and
\vec{V}-\Phi=(Y-\Phi,Z-\Phi)
in the following proof. We have

\displaystyle\mathbf{T}(|\widetilde{\nabla}Y|)\widetilde{\nabla}Y-\mathbf{T}(|\widetilde{\nabla}Z|)\widetilde{\nabla}Z-J(\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi)\widetilde{\nabla}(Y-Z)=\mathbf{R}=\mathbf{R}_{1}+\mathbf{R}_{2}+\mathbf{R}_{3}+\mathbf{R}_{4},

with

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{1}=(\mathbf{T}(|\widetilde{\nabla}Y|)-\mathbf{T}(\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi))\widetilde{\nabla}(Y-Z)+(\mathbf{T}(|\widetilde{\nabla}Y|)-\mathbf{T}(|\widetilde{\nabla}Z|))\widetilde{\nabla}(Z-\Phi),

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{2}=\big(\mathbf{T}(|\widetilde{\nabla}Y|)-\mathbf{T}(|\widetilde{\nabla}Z|)-\mathbf{T}^{\prime}(|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|)(|\widetilde{\nabla}Y|-|\widetilde{\nabla}Z|)\big)\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi,

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{3}=(\mathbf{T}^{\prime}(|\widetilde{\nabla}Y|-\mathbf{T}^{\prime}(|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|))(|\widetilde{\nabla}Y|-|\widetilde{\nabla}Z|),

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{4}=\big(\mathbf{T}^{\prime}(|\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi|)(|\widetilde{\nabla}Y|-|\widetilde{\nabla}Z|)\big)\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi-J(\widetilde{\nabla}\Phi)\widetilde{\nabla}(Y-Z).

By applying Remark
6.19
with
\mathbf{G}=G(\Phi(\widehat{\boldsymbol{x}})-\Phi(\widehat{\boldsymbol{y}}))
, we have

\|(N_{2}(Y,\Phi)-N_{2}(Z,\Phi))\|_{C^{\kappa}}\lesssim(1+m_{\Phi})\|\mathbf{R}\|_{\dot{C}^{\kappa}}.

By Lemma
6.5
we have

\|\mathbf{R}\|_{C^{\kappa}}\lesssim t^{-\kappa}\mathfrak{M}_{1}\|Y-Z\|_{Z_{T}}(\|\vec{V}-\Phi\|_{Z_{T}}+T^{\kappa}M_{\Phi})(1+\|\vec{V}-\Phi\|_{Z_{T}}+m_{\Phi}).

This yields that

\displaystyle\|(N_{2}(Y,\Phi)-N_{2}(Z,\Phi))\|_{C^{\kappa}}

\displaystyle\lesssim t^{-\kappa}\mathfrak{M}_{1}\|Y-Z\|_{Z_{T}}(\|\vec{V}-\Phi\|_{Z_{T}}+T^{\kappa}M_{\Phi})(1+\|\vec{V}-\Phi\|_{Z_{T}}+m_{\Phi})^{2}.

For higher order derivatives, we use (
5.20
), integrating by parts and taking derivatives on
\mathbf{R}
to obtain

\displaystyle\|(N_{2}(Y,\Phi)-N_{2}(Z,\Phi))\|_{C^{m+\kappa}}

\displaystyle\lesssim t^{-m-\kappa}\mathfrak{M}_{1}\|Y-Z\|_{Z_{T}}(\|\vec{V}-\Phi\|_{Z_{T}}+T^{\kappa}M_{\Phi})(1+\|\vec{V}-\Phi\|_{Z_{T}}+m_{\Phi}+T^{\kappa}M_{\Phi})^{m+3}.

This completes the proof of the lemma.

Finally, for
N_{3}(\Phi)
, since
\Phi
is smooth, it is straightforward to obtain the following estimate.

Lemma 6.22

Let
N_{3}(\Phi)
be defined in (
5.6
), it holds

\displaystyle\|N_{3}(\Phi)\|_{C^{m+\kappa}}\lesssim\mathfrak{M}_{1}M_{\Phi}(1+M_{\Phi})^{m+2}.
